## Lessons
- Size packages so one agent finishes well within a session: codegen should have been ABI+mangling / lowering / driver+tests from the start (the pp engine ended up split the same way). Prefer 3 small briefs over 1 large one; a brief that lists >5 deliverables is too big.

| WP-1058 | x86-64 glibc `_Float128` vs `__float128` (WP-1000 follow-up, keeps them distinct per decision (B)): kiln's `__GNUC__` < 13 makes glibc <bits/floatn.h> reach `typedef __float128 _Float128;`, which kiln already drops in a system header as a no-op extension (spec/types.md), so glibc's `*f128` functions (`logf128`, ...) stay `_Float128` functions while LLVM's llvm/Support/float128.h uses `__float128` under `__clang__`; `apply(logf128, v)` with a `float128 (*)(float128)` parameter fails (ConstantFolding.cpp). Candidate fix: as an extension, accept the conversion between function (pointer) types that differ only in `_Float128` vs `__float128` in a system-header declaration, or honour that exact typedef under clang compat; xfail basic.extended.fp/float128-glibc-typedef-x86.cpp | WP-1000 | done (#493) |
